## Separate gates (not folded into software state)

### software

- PRD requires: PRD 1.4 states 1-3: internal software completion and installed development reconstruction; Z0..Z5 requirements of sections 3.3-9 and 11.2-11.4
- internally achievable: All items with class NATIVE_*/INSTALLED_RECONSTRUCTION/PUBLIC_CI/STATIC_CHECK/REGRESSION on disposable loopback with the pinned Auth and embedded PostgreSQL
- external: none (missing internal implementation is INCOMPLETE)

### independentQualification

- PRD requires: PRD 3.6, 3.7, 10.2: independently governed semantic specification, checker and hidden population; >=32 families x >=8 fresh cases per engine; >=30 independent institution/period blocks
- internally achievable: Public development fixtures only (counted as public development)
- external: Protected population, registrar, hidden cases and custody: EP-01, EP-02, EP-05

### matchedOnOffComparison

- PRD requires: PRD 10.3, 10.5: full candidate with all five engines disabled against B_XY_release with the same preparation resources; five ablations; no product of ratios; PRD 5.6, 6.6, 7.6, 8.5, 8.6, 9.6 comparisons
- internally achievable: Registration of the ablation switches and cost meters
- external: Qualified B_XY_release and its equipped actors: EP-04; registered campaign values: EP-05; absent baseline is BLOCKED, never defeated

### hundredXAndEconomics

- PRD requires: PRD 1.2, 10.1-10.7: 100x independently accepted material work, simultaneous 95% bounds, financial margin, 36-month H2, net attributable owner value
- internally achievable: Meter collection and arithmetic of the bounds on supplied data
- external: Registered epsilons, weights, load grid, second institution cohort, mature outcomes, material margin: EP-05, EP-08, EP-07

### activation

- PRD requires: PRD 1.4 states 5 and 9, 11.9: independently governed protected admission and actual production activation
- internally achievable: Nothing: no push, PR, merge, deploy, grant, signer, counterparty contact or financial commitment
- external: Protected admission authority and activation authority: EP-09

## External request packets (drafted, not sent)

### EP-01

- needed: An independently governed registrar (semantic specification, checker, hidden fresh population, custody) producing >=32 structural families x >=8 fresh cases for each of Z1-Z5, with all critical falsifier, positive-rate and infeasibility checks
- from: an independent governance body outside the builder/coordinator
- requirement ids: CC-R04, Z1-R48, Z2-R48, Z3-R48, Z4-R48, Z5-R48
- why it cannot be built internally: PRD 3.7 and 3.6: builders cannot administer their own protected population; a second chat or language does not establish independence

### EP-02

- needed: >=40 previously unseen Z4 function definitions/compositions revealed only after the general compiler is frozen, with an exact reference interpreter
- from: independent registrar
- requirement ids: Z4-R09, Z4-R12
- why it cannot be built internally: PRD 8.4: the definitions must be withheld from the builder; the 32 public programmes cannot play this role

### EP-03

- needed: Independent security review and qualification of the adopted Nova cut, parameters, setup/transparency profile and the soundness claim
- from: independent cryptographic reviewer
- requirement ids: Z4-R05, Z4-R11
- why it cannot be built internally: PRD 8.3-8.4: no audit or security qualification is assumed; production security qualification is a separate blocked/unknown state

### EP-04

- needed: A qualified B_XY_release (all S/P/M/R/V/X/Y methods with equipped libraries) and configured comparison actors running on frozen versions, rights, capital and prices
- from: owner of the qualified X/Y release and the campaign registrar
- requirement ids: Z1-R16, Z2-R20, Z3-R16, Z5-R19
- why it cannot be built internally: PRD 2.1, 10.3: an absent B_XY_release is BLOCKED comparison; it cannot be constructed by the builder without being the comparator

### EP-05

- needed: Independently registered positive epsilons, weights, task mix, arrivals, load grid and statistical procedure; a second independently administered institution/cohort; >=30 independent institution/period blocks (power-analysed)
- from: independent campaign registrar
- requirement ids: Z1-R48, Z2-R48, Z3-R48, Z4-R48, Z5-R48
- why it cannot be built internally: PRD 1.2, 3.7, 10.2: registered before exposure and independent of the builder

### EP-06

- needed: An independently admitted environment-fairness basis and any original S5 renewable-resource meaning for a Z3 native positive with renewal
- from: S5/S8 owners (admission authority)
- requirement ids: Z3-R11, Z3-R12
- why it cannot be built internally: PRD 7.4: fairness and renewal need an independently admitted external basis; a builder may not invent a renewal or fairness premise; handoff 28 reports that no original S5 renewable meaning exists (not independently verified here)

### EP-07

- needed: Registered real verifier demand N and reuse horizon for the Z4 cost crossover
- from: campaign registrar / owners of real demand
- requirement ids: Z4-R16
- why it cannot be built internally: PRD 8.5: do not invent repeated demand; the registration has eligibleReuseN 0

### EP-08

- needed: Mature S7 outcomes (H0/H1/H2 at 36 months), registered material decision-payoff margin and net attributable owner value accounting
- from: S7 economic owners / finance campaign
- requirement ids: CC-G01
- why it cannot be built internally: PRD 10.6: time-dependent real outcomes and independent registration

### EP-09

- needed: Protected admission authority and production activation authority for any Z engine
- from: independent governance and the user
- requirement ids: CC-G02
- why it cannot be built internally: PRD 1.4, 11.9: no earlier state implies admission or activation; no builder-created authority

### EP-10

- needed: A host with >=16 GiB aggregate simultaneous peak memory for original-episode qualification (current verify host 7.9 GB)
- from: infrastructure owner
- requirement ids: CC-R10
- why it cannot be built internally: PRD 11.2: the development host is not a 16 GiB qualification receipt; provisioning is not authorized for builders
